Implement the following plan:

# Enriching Agentic Journey Captures for Universe Models

## Context

Cohezion's compound engineering loop has sophisticated infrastructure for tracking agent journeys through the 12-parameter axiomatic universe, but the pipeline is operationally incomplete. The core measurement -- cohezion (the overlap between internal intent and external manifestation) -- defaults to 0.5 everywhere because nothing computes it. Trajectories are truncated to their last point, losing the temporal dynamics that reveal HIHO stability patterns. The RL policy trains on synthetic rewards instead of real journey data. Feedback loops between detection and action are open. The universe simulation engine (25K LOC) sits dormant, disconnected from the compound executor.

### Ontological Foundation

The 12 parameters that define agent state emerge from successive application of the **quadrature principle** -- splitting awareness into orthogonal perspectives until we arrive at 4 fabrics, each with 3 observable dimensions:

| Fabric | Parameters | What It Captures |
|--------|-----------|-----------------|
| **Space** | spatial_x, spatial_y, spatial_z | Geometric position in possibility space |
| **Field** | physics, biology, field | Force interactions and domain coupling |
| **Control** | logic, quantum, control | Information flow, causation, entropy |
| **Precipitation** | temporal, novelty, precipitation | Manifestation of latent intent into reality |

**Spin** is the most basic principal component -- rotation + precession as the fundamental unit of information. When spin phases align, cohezion increases. When they diverge, symmetry breaks and new structure precipitates from the awareness of nothing.

**HIHO (Half-In-Half-Out)** is the stability principle: maximum cohezion occurs at exactly 0.5 overlap between internal state and external environment. This is not a constraint but an attractor -- the sweet spot where exploration and exploitation balance, where symmetry can break in either direction. All 12 parameters target HIHO at 0.5.

This plan closes the open loops progressively across 7 phases. Each phase is independently valuable and compounds into the next. The guiding principle: **the minimal observation that unlocks the most downstream value**.

---

## Phase 1: Populate Real Cohezion Scores (1 session)

**Goal**: Compute and store a real `metrics["coherence"]` value in the executor so every downstream consumer receives actual cohezion signal instead of the hardcoded HIHO default.

**Why first**: This is the single highest-leverage fix. JourneyTracker, DegradationDetector, ExperienceEncoder, and SkillRefiner all read the cohezion metric and all get 0.5 today because nothing sets it. The entire system believes it's perpetually at perfect HIHO equilibrium -- blind to its own spin alignment.

**Data flow change**: After Step 5.5 (alignment analysis) in the executor, compute cohezion from available signals: success flag (did reality precipitate as intended?), anomaly_score (spin misalignment detection), and alignment intent_match (quadrature consensus with human request). Store as `metrics["coherence"]`.

**Formula**:
```python
# Cohezion = overlap between agent's internal intent and precipitated result
components = [0.7 if success else 0.2]                     # precipitation success
components.append(1.0 - metrics.get("anomaly_score", 0.5)) # spin alignment (inverse anomaly)
if alignment_data := metrics.get("alignment", {}):
    components.append(alignment_data.get("intent_match", 0.5))  # quadrature consensus
metrics["coherence"] = sum(components) / len(components)
```

**Files modified**:
- `src/cohezion/compound/executor.py` (~15 lines inserted between Steps 5.5 and 6, around line 557)

**Tests** (in `tests/compound/test_executor.py`):
1. Cohezion populated and >0.5 on successful precipitation
2. Cohezion populated and <0.5 on failed precipitation
3. Cohezion incorporates anomaly_score (spin misalignment)
4. Cohezion incorporates alignment intent_match when quadrature analysis available
5. Cohezion still computed (from precipitation alone) when no detectors configured

**Compounds into**: Every subsequent phase. Without real cohezion, all downstream consumers train on noise -- the system can't distinguish HIHO stability from HIHO ignorance.

---

## Phase 2: Preserve Full Trajectory Statistics (1 session)

**Goal**: Replace the `state_trajectory[-1]` truncation in ExperienceCollector with trajectory summary statistics so the VAE can learn the temporal dynamics of symmetry breaking and HIHO drift across all 4 fabrics.

**Why second**: With real cohezion from Phase 1, trajectories now carry real signal about spin alignment over time. But `experience_collector.py` line ~104 discards the entire trajectory history, keeping only the final 12-parameter point. The VAE cannot learn drift patterns -- it sees a snapshot, not the process of precipitation.

**Data flow change**: In the collector's normalization methods, compute `trajectory_smoothness` (stability of spin alignment across the 4 fabrics: 1.0 - mean absolute parameter changes) and `trajectory_convergence` (HIHO convergence: 1.0 - std of last 3 cohezion values) from the full `state_trajectory` list. Store these in the metric slots currently occupied by low-signal fields (`convergence_trend`, `quality_score`).

**Files modified**:
- `src/cohezion/flume/experience_collector.py` -- Update `_normalize_parquet_row` and `_normalize_surreal_row` to compute trajectory stats from full `state_trajectory`
- `src/cohezion/flume/experience_encoder.py` -- Update `METRIC_KEYS` to replace `convergence_trend`/`quality_score` with `trajectory_smoothness`/`trajectory_convergence`

**Tests** (in `tests/flume/`):
1. Multi-point trajectory produces non-zero smoothness/convergence across fabrics
2. Single-point trajectory gracefully defaults (smoothness=1.0, no symmetry breaking observed)
3. Encoded 256D vector has non-zero trajectory metric slots
4. Empty trajectory still produces valid 256D vector (backward compat)
5. SurrealDB rows also compute trajectory stats

**Compounds into**: Phase 5 (VAE training needs temporal signal to learn fabric dynamics), Phase 4 (phi_score needs these values for trajectory quality).

---

## Phase 3: Close the Degradation Feedback Loop (1 session)

**Goal**: Make the executor act on DegradationDetector alerts -- enter a degradation mode that conserves resources when cohezion collapses below the HIHO stability band.

**Why third**: Phases 1-2 gave us real cohezion and temporal awareness. The DegradationDetector now produces meaningful alerts when cohezion drifts outside the HIHO band (0.4-0.6), but the executor logs them and does nothing (line ~627). This is the first feedback loop to close: detection already works, we just need a behavioral response. This is spin precession correction -- when the system detects misalignment, it adjusts its own trajectory.

**Data flow change**: After `check_degradation()` in Step 7.5, if a CRITICAL cohezion alert fires (spin alignment collapsed), set `self._degradation_mode = True` and `metrics["execution_degraded"] = True`. At the start of `execute_task`, if in degradation mode, skip expensive optional steps (alignment analysis, pattern extraction) to conserve tokens and reduce the surface area for further symmetry breaking. Exit degradation mode when cohezion returns to the HIHO band.

**Files modified**:
- `src/cohezion/compound/executor.py` -- Add `_degradation_mode` flag, check it at execution start, set/clear it based on HIHO band violations

**Tests** (in `tests/compound/test_executor.py`):
1. `_degradation_mode` set on CRITICAL cohezion alert (outside HIHO band)
2. In degraded mode, alignment analysis is skipped
3. In degraded mode, pattern extraction is skipped
4. Degradation mode clears when cohezion returns to HIHO stability
5. `execution_degraded` flag appears in ExecutionResult.metrics

**Compounds into**: Phase 6 (RetrospectionEngine uses degradation events as learning signals -- understanding when and why spin alignment collapses). Also prevents generating bad training data for Phase 5.

---

## Phase 4: Compute Real Smoothness/Convergence for phi_score (1 session)

**Goal**: Replace the hardcoded `smoothness=0.5, convergence=0.5` in JourneyTracker's phi_score calculation with actual trajectory-derived values that measure how smoothly spin alignment evolves across the 4 fabrics.

**Why fourth**: The JourneyTracker has a `compute_trajectory_quality` method (lines 426-471) that computes real smoothness and convergence from point lists, but `track_execution` never calls it -- it always passes HIHO defaults. With real cohezion (Phase 1), we can now measure meaningful trajectory quality: how smoothly does the agent navigate the 12-parameter manifold? Does it converge toward HIHO or oscillate chaotically?

**Data flow change**: Add a `_recent_points` buffer (capped at 20) to JourneyTracker. On each `track_execution`, append the new point, then call `compute_trajectory_quality` on the buffer to get real smoothness (fabric stability) and convergence (HIHO approach rate) for phi_score.

**Files modified**:
- `src/cohezion/compound/journey_tracker.py` -- Add `_recent_points` buffer in `__init__`, update `track_execution` to maintain buffer and compute real values from trajectory history

**Tests** (in `tests/compound/test_journey_tracker.py`):
1. phi_score differs from hardcoded calculation after 5+ executions
2. Consistent tasks (stable spin) produce smoothness > 0.7
3. Divergent tasks (symmetry breaking) produce smoothness < 0.5
4. Stable cohezion values (HIHO convergence) produce convergence > 0.7
5. Buffer caps at window size (20 max)

**Compounds into**: Phase 5 (phi_score is a key metric in ExperienceEncoder dim 12 -- the VAE learns what good trajectories look like). Phase 6 (RetrospectionEngine uses phi_score to gate refinement -- only refine when the trajectory quality warrants it).

---

## Phase 5: Train VAE on Real Experience Data (1 session)

**Goal**: Create an end-to-end pipeline connecting ExperienceCollector -> ExperienceEncoder -> FlumeVAETrainer so the VAE learns the actual latent structure of agentic experience -- the 256D encoding that captures spin alignment, fabric dynamics, and precipitation patterns from real executions.

**Why fifth**: Phases 1-4 enriched the data. Now ExperienceCollector produces records with real cohezion, trajectory smoothness across fabrics, and phi_scores reflecting actual HIHO dynamics. The ExperienceEncoder maps these into 256D vectors (12D axiomatic + 12D metrics + 5D operation type + 227D semantic fingerprint). The FlumeVAETrainer trains on 256D vectors. The pieces exist but have never been connected -- the VAE currently trains on synthetic Gaussian noise instead of real symmetry-breaking events.

**Data flow change**: Add an `ExperienceDataset` class (torch Dataset wrapping collector+encoder) and a `train_from_experiences()` convenience method on FlumeVAETrainer. Falls back to synthetic data if <10 real samples. The trained VAE's latent space will then reflect actual execution patterns -- the manifold of spin alignments, fabric couplings, and precipitation outcomes that agents actually traverse.

**Files modified**:
- `src/cohezion/flume/dataset.py` -- Add `ExperienceDataset` class
- `src/cohezion/flume/training.py` -- Add `train_from_experiences()` method

**Tests** (in `tests/flume/`):
1. ExperienceDataset produces 256D tensors from mock collector output
2. Empty collector returns synthetic fallback
3. `train_from_experiences()` completes 1 epoch without error
4. Trained VAE has lower reconstruction MSE than untrained (it learned structure)
5. Same experience dict always produces same 256D encoding (deterministic)

**Compounds into**: Phase 7 (universe simulation uses VAE latent space -- agents navigate a learned manifold, not a random one). Future: VAE reconstruction error as spin anomaly detector (high error = unprecedented symmetry breaking event).

---

## Phase 6: Connect RetrospectionEngine to Live Executions (1 session)

**Goal**: Make RetrospectionEngine analyze actual ExecutionResult objects and use its output to gate skill refinement -- closing the full compound loop from precipitation to learning.

**Why sixth**: RetrospectionEngine currently only reads static KEY_LEARNINGS.md. Meanwhile, the SkillRefiner appends refinements without knowing if they help. With Phases 1-5 providing real cohezion metrics, fabric dynamics, and HIHO convergence data, we can close the full compound loop: **Execute -> Measure cohezion -> Detect HIHO drift -> Analyze via retrospection -> Refine skill -> Execute again**. This is the quadrature principle applied to learning itself -- 4 perspectives (execution quality, spin alignment, fabric stability, precipitation success) must align before a refinement is accepted.

**Data flow change**: Add `RetrospectionEngine.analyze_execution_result(result, skill_name)` that extracts compound insights from live data. In executor.py, add Step 7.3 after skill refinement: call retrospection, use its `should_refine` output to gate refinement (only refine when cohezion and phi_score warrant it -- don't learn from chaotic trajectories). Pass retrospection context to SkillRefiner.

**Files modified**:
- `src/cohezion/core/compound/retrospection.py` -- Add `analyze_execution_result()` method
- `src/cohezion/compound/executor.py` -- Add Step 7.3 calling retrospection, gate refinement
- `src/cohezion/compound/skill_refiner.py` -- Accept optional `retrospection_context` parameter

**Tests** (in `tests/compound/`):
1. `analyze_execution_result` extracts insights from ExecutionResult
2. Low cohezion execution (HIHO violation) returns `should_refine=False`
3. Executor calls retrospection after execution (when configured)
4. Retrospection insights appear in skill refinement section
5. Degraded execution produces investigation recommendation

**Compounds into**: Phase 7 (universe simulation uses retrospection insights to understand why certain regions of the manifold produce poor precipitation). This completes the compound loop.

---

## Phase 7: Bridge Journeys to Universe Simulation (1 session)

**Goal**: Connect enriched journey data to the UniverseSimulationEngine so it tracks real agent journeys through the 12-parameter manifold -- spin alignment, fabric coupling, and precipitation events from actual compound executions.

**Why last**: Phases 1-6 built the complete instrumented pipeline: real cohezion, trajectory dynamics across fabrics, self-correcting HIHO drift, meaningful phi_scores, a VAE trained on real experience, and retrospection that gates learning. The UniverseSimulationEngine has a rich data model (UniverseJourney, AxiomaticState across 4 fabrics, precipitation records) but is disconnected from the compound executor. This phase bridges them -- agents finally leave footprints in the universe they inhabit.

**Data flow change**: Create `UniverseBridge` adapter that converts JourneyTracker's TrajectoryPoints (12-parameter vectors) into UniverseSimulationEngine's AxiomaticState model (organized by fabric). Hook into executor at start (create journey -- symmetry breaking from nothing), Step 9 (add trajectory point -- spin evolution), and completion (finalize journey -- precipitation of result into reality).

**Files modified**:
- New: `src/cohezion/compound/universe_bridge.py` -- Adapter between compound executor and universe engine
- `src/cohezion/compound/executor.py` -- Accept optional `universe_bridge`, call it at execution lifecycle points

**Tests** (in `tests/compound/`):
1. Execution creates a UniverseJourney (symmetry breaking event)
2. JourneyTracker point correctly maps to AxiomaticState across 4 fabrics
3. Successful execution completes journey with phi_score and precipitation record
4. Bridge gracefully no-ops if engine unavailable
5. Real cohezion from Phase 1 flows through to universe trajectory (HIHO preserved end-to-end)

**Compounds into**: Future work -- RL environment seeding with real trajectories (replacing FlumeNav-v0's synthetic spin), 12-parameter manifold visualization showing agent paths through fabric space, replay of historical journeys for training, and the ultimate goal: agents that learn to navigate toward regions of the manifold where precipitation is most aligned with intent.

## Complete Data Flow After All 7 Phases

```
execute_fn(guidance) -> (output, metrics)
  |
  +-- P1: Real cohezion score -> metrics["coherence"]
  |     |   (spin alignment between intent and precipitation)
  |     |
  |     +-- P3: DegradationDetector monitors HIHO band
  |     |     +-- Enters/exits degradation mode on cohezion collapse
  |     |
  |     +-- P4: JourneyTracker uses real cohezion for phi_score
  |           +-- Real smoothness (fabric stability) / convergence (HIHO approach)
  |
  +-- P2: ExperienceCollector preserves trajectory dynamics
  |     +-- trajectory_smoothness, trajectory_convergence across fabrics
  |
  +-- P5: ExperienceDataset -> VAE trains on real spin/fabric data
  |     +-- Latent space reflects actual precipitation patterns
  |
  +-- P6: RetrospectionEngine analyzes live ExecutionResult
  |     +-- Gates SkillRefiner via quadrature (refine only when warranted)
  |
  +-- P7: UniverseBridge -> simulation engine
        +-- Real 12-parameter trajectories through fabric space
        +-- Agents leave footprints in the universe they inhabit
```

After each phase, verify with:

uv run pytest tests/compound/ tests/flume/ -v  # Module tests pass
uv run pytest tests/ -q                         # No regressions in full suite
```

3. Verify phi_score uses real smoothness/convergence from fabric dynamics
4. Trigger degradation mode and verify self-correction (spin precession adjustment)
5. Verify ExperienceCollector preserves full trajectory stats across fabrics
6. Train VAE on collected experiences, verify it learns precipitation structure
7. Verify universe journey created with real 12-parameter trajectory data

   246→            task_description: Description of the task
   247→            operation_type: Type of operation (generate, analyze, etc.)
   248→            project: Project name for scoped search
   249→            top_k: Number of skill suggestions to return
   250→
   251→        Returns:
   252→            List of (skill_name, score) tuples, sorted by score (highest first)
   253→        """
   254→        try:
   255→            from cohezion.compound.skill_selector import SkillSelector
   256→
   257→            selector = SkillSelector(self.mcp_client)
   258→            suggestions = selector.select_skills(
   259→                task_description,
   260→                operation_type,
   261→                project=project,
   262→                top_k=top_k,
   263→            )
   264→
   265→            logger.info(
   266→                "Suggested %d skills for task: %s",
   267→                len(suggestions),
   268→                ", ".join(s.skill_name for s in suggestions),
   269→            )
   270→
   271→            return [(s.skill_name, s.composite_score) for s in suggestions]
   272→        except Exception as e:
   273→            logger.warning(
   274→                "Error suggesting skills: %s. Returning empty list.",
   275→                e,
   276→                exc_info=True,
   277→            )
   278→            return []
   279→
   280→    def execute_task(
   281→        self,
   282→        task_description: str,
   283→        skill_name: str,
   284→        operation_type: str,
   285→        execute_fn: Callable,
   286→        project: str = "cohezion",
   287→        human_request: str | None = None,
   288→    ) -> ExecutionResult:
   289→        """Execute a compound task with vault logging.
   290→
   291→        Args:
   292→            task_description: What the task does
   293→            skill_name: Name of the skill being executed
   294→            operation_type: Type of operation
   295→                (generate, analyze, search, transform, persist)
   296→            execute_fn: Callable that executes the task, returns (output, metrics)
   297→                Can optionally use self.token_client if available
   298→            project: Project name for vault logging
   299→            human_request: Optional raw request text for alignment analysis
   300→
   301→        Returns:
   302→            ExecutionResult with success status, output, metrics, vault paths,
   303→            and token_metrics if TokenEfficientClient was used
   304→        """
   305→        start_time = datetime.now()
   306→        start_seconds = time.time()
   307→
   308→        # Create execution context
   309→        ctx = ExecutionContext(
   310→            project=project,
   311→            skill_name=skill_name,
   312→            task_description=task_description,
   313→            operation_type=operation_type,
   314→            start_time=start_time,
   315→            mcp_client=self.mcp_client,
   316→        )
   317→
   318→        logger.info(
   319→            "Executing task: %s (operation=%s, skill=%s)",
   320→            task_description,
   321→            operation_type,
   322→            skill_name,
   323→        )
   324→
   325→        # Step 1: Get experience guidance
   326→        guidance = self.get_experience_guidance(task_description, project)
   327→        logger.debug("Experience guidance: %s", guidance)
   328→
   329→        # Step 1.5: Parse request for alignment analysis (if enabled)
   330→        parsed_request = None
   331→        alignment_patterns = None
   332→        if self._enable_alignment_analysis and self.alignment_analyzer:
   333→            try:
   334→                request_text = human_request or task_description
   335→                parsed_request = self.alignment_analyzer.parse_request(request_text)
   336→                alignment_patterns = self.alignment_analyzer.query_alignment_patterns(
   337→                    task_description, project
   338→                )
   339→                logger.debug(
   340→                    "Parsed request: intent=%s (confidence=%.2f), "
   341→                    "%d constraints, %d criteria",
   342→                    parsed_request.intent.value,
   343→                    parsed_request.intent_confidence,
   344→                    len(parsed_request.constraints),
   345→                    len(parsed_request.criteria),
   346→                )
   347→            except Exception as e:
   348→                logger.debug(
   349→                    "Request alignment parsing failed (non-blocking): %s", e, exc_info=True
   350→                )
   351→
   352→        # Step 2: Log execution start
   353→        experiment_path = self.logger.log_execution_start(ctx)
   354→
   355→        # Step 3: Check input via guardrails
   356→        success = False
   357→        output = ""
   358→        metrics: dict[str, Any] = {}
   359→        token_metrics: dict[str, Any] | None = None
   360→        error_msg = ""
   361→
   362→        # Check input against guardrails if enabled
   363→        if self.guardrail_pipeline:
   364→            guard_context = {
   365→                "skill_name": skill_name,
   366→                "operation_type": operation_type,
   367→                "task_description": task_description,
   368→            }
   369→            input_check = _run_async_guardrail(
   370→                self.guardrail_pipeline.check_input(
   371→                    task_description, guard_context
   372→                )
   373→            )
   374→            if input_check and input_check.action == GuardrailAction.BLOCK:
   375→                error_msg = f"Input blocked by guardrails: {input_check.reason}"
   376→                output = f"Error: {error_msg}"
   377→                metrics = {"error": error_msg, "blocked_by_guardrails": True}
   378→                logger.warning("Task input blocked: %s", input_check.reason)
   379→                # Log execution result and return
   380→                self.logger.log_execution_result(
   381→                    experiment_path=experiment_path,
   382→                    success=False,
   383→                    output=output,
   384→                    metrics=metrics,
   385→                )
   386→                return ExecutionResult(
   387→                    success=False,
   388→                    output=output,
   389→                    metrics=metrics,
   390→                    duration_seconds=time.time() - start_seconds,
   391→                    vault_experiment_path=experiment_path,
   392→                )
   393→
   394→        # Capture token metrics before execution (if token_client available)
   395→        token_metrics_before = None
   396→        if self.token_client:
   397→            token_metrics_before = self.token_client.get_metrics()
   398→
   399→        try:
   400→            output, metrics = execute_fn(guidance)
   401→            success = True
   402→            logger.info("Task completed successfully")
   403→        except Exception as e:
   404→            error_msg = str(e)
   405→            output = f"Error: {error_msg}"
   406→            metrics = {"error": error_msg}
   407→            logger.error("Task failed: %s", error_msg, exc_info=True)
   408→
   409→        # Capture token metrics after execution (if token_client available)
   410→        if self.token_client:
   411→            token_metrics_after = self.token_client.get_metrics()
   412→            token_metrics = self._compute_token_delta(
   413→                token_metrics_before, token_metrics_after
   414→            )
   415→            logger.debug("Token metrics: %s", token_metrics)
   416→
   417→        # Check output via guardrails if successful
   418→        if success and self.guardrail_pipeline:
   419→            guard_context = {
   420→                "skill_name": skill_name,
   421→                "operation_type": operation_type,
   422→                "task_description": task_description,
   423→            }
   424→            output_check = _run_async_guardrail(
   425→                self.guardrail_pipeline.check_output(
   426→                    output, guard_context
   427→                )
   428→            )
   429→            if output_check:
   430→                if output_check.action == GuardrailAction.BLOCK:
   431→                    output = "[Output blocked by content filter]"
   432→                    success = False
   433→                    metrics["output_blocked_by_guardrails"] = True
   434→                    logger.warning("Task output blocked: %s", output_check.reason)
   435→                elif output_check.action == GuardrailAction.SANITIZE:
   436→                    if output_check.modified_input:
   437→                        output = output_check.modified_input
   438→                        metrics["output_sanitized_by_guardrails"] = True
   439→                        logger.debug("Task output sanitized")
   440→
   441→        duration_seconds = time.time() - start_seconds
   442→        metrics["duration_seconds"] = duration_seconds
   443→
   444→        # Step 4: Log execution results
   445→        self.logger.log_execution_result(
   446→            experiment_path=experiment_path,
   447→            success=success,
   448→            output=output,
   449→            metrics=metrics,
   450→        )
   451→
   452→        # Step 5: Detect anomalies (non-blocking)
   453→        decision_paths = []
   454→        try:
   455→            from cohezion.compound.inflection_detector import Severity
   456→            temp_result = ExecutionResult(
   457→                success=success,
   458→                output=output,
   459→                metrics=metrics,
   460→                duration_seconds=duration_seconds,
   461→                token_metrics=token_metrics,
   462→            )
   463→            anomaly = self.inflection_detector.detect_anomaly(temp_result)
   464→            metrics["anomaly_severity"] = anomaly.severity.value
   465→            metrics["anomaly_score"] = anomaly.score
   466→            logger.debug(
   467→                "Anomaly detection: severity=%s, score=%.2f, issues=%s",
   468→                anomaly.severity.value,
   469→                anomaly.score,
   470→                anomaly.issues,
   471→            )
   472→            # Log critical inflection points to vault
   473→            if anomaly.severity == Severity.CRITICAL:
   474→                logger.warning(
   475→                    "Critical inflection point detected: %s issues",
   476→                    len(anomaly.issues),
   477→                )
   478→                try:
   479→                    decision_path = self.log_inflection_point(
   480→                        title=f"Critical anomaly in {skill_name}",
   481→                        context=f"Task: {task_description}\nIssues: {'; '.join(anomaly.issues)}",
   482→                        decision="Re-execution recommended",
   483→                        rationale=f"Quality score {anomaly.score:.2f}, {anomaly.recommendations[0] if anomaly.recommendations else 'Investigate issues'}",
   484→                        project=project,
   485→                    )
   486→                    if decision_path:
   487→                        decision_paths.append(decision_path)
   488→                except Exception as e:
   489→                    logger.debug(
   490→                        "Failed to log inflection point (non-blocking): %s", e
   491→                    )
   492→        except Exception as e:
   493→            logger.debug(
   494→                "Anomaly detection failed (non-blocking): %s", e, exc_info=True
   495→            )
   496→
   497→        # Step 5.5: Analyze request-execution alignment (if enabled)
   498→        if self._enable_alignment_analysis and self.alignment_analyzer and parsed_request:
   499→            try:
   500→                from cohezion.compound.inflection_detector import Severity
   501→
   502→                temp_result = ExecutionResult(
   503→                    success=success,
   504→                    output=output,
   505→                    metrics=metrics,
   506→                    duration_seconds=duration_seconds,
   507→                    token_metrics=token_metrics,
   508→                )
   509→
   510→                # Get anomaly analysis if available
   511→                anomaly_analysis = None
   512→                if "anomaly_severity" in metrics:
   513→                    # Create a minimal anomaly object for alignment analysis
   514→                    from cohezion.compound.inflection_detector import AnomalyDetection
   515→
   516→                    severity_val = metrics.get("anomaly_severity", "info")
   517→                    severity_enum = Severity(severity_val)
   518→                    anomaly_analysis = AnomalyDetection(
   519→                        severity=severity_enum,
   520→                        score=metrics.get("anomaly_score", 0.0),
   521→                        issues=metrics.get("anomaly_issues", []),
   522→                        recommendations=metrics.get("anomaly_recommendations", []),
   523→                        should_reexecute=False,
   524→                    )
   525→
   526→                alignment = self.alignment_analyzer.analyze_alignment(
   527→                    parsed_request, temp_result, operation_type, anomaly_analysis
   528→                )
   529→
   530→                # Log alignment to vault if high misalignment
   531→                if alignment.misalignment_score > 0.3:
   532→                    vault_path = self.alignment_analyzer.log_alignment_to_vault(
   533→                        parsed_request, alignment, project
   534→                    )
   535→                    if vault_path:
   536→                        decision_paths.append(vault_path)
   537→                        logger.debug("Logged alignment analysis: %s", vault_path)
   538→
   539→                # Add alignment metrics to result
   540→                metrics["alignment"] = {
   541→                    "misalignment_score": alignment.misalignment_score,
   542→                    "intent_match": alignment.intent_match_score,
   543→                    "constraint_satisfaction": alignment.constraint_satisfaction,
   544→                    "criteria_satisfaction": alignment.criteria_satisfaction,
   545→                    "violations_count": len(alignment.violations),
   546→                    "failures_count": len(alignment.failures),
   547→                    "issues_count": len(alignment.issues),
   548→                    "should_retry": alignment.should_retry,
   549→                }
   550→                logger.debug("Alignment analysis: %s", metrics["alignment"])
   551→            except Exception as e:
   552→                logger.debug(
   553→                    "Request alignment analysis failed (non-blocking): %s",
   554→                    e,
   555→                    exc_info=True,
   556→                )
   557→
   558→        # Step 6: If successful, extract patterns
   559→        if success and experiment_path:
   560→            try:
   561→                pattern_path = self.logger.extract_execution_pattern(
   562→                    source_path=experiment_path,
   563→                    pattern_name=f"{skill_name}_{operation_type}_success",
   564→                    description=f"Successful execution pattern for {skill_name} "
   565→                    f"operation: {operation_type}. "
   566→                    f"Task: {task_description[:100]}",
   567→                    code_example=f"Result metrics: {json.dumps(metrics, indent=2)}",
   568→                    domain="compound-engineering",
   569→                )
   570→                if pattern_path:
   571→                    decision_paths.append(pattern_path)
   572→            except Exception as e:
   573→                logger.warning("Failed to extract pattern: %s", e, exc_info=True)
   574→
   575→        # Step 7: Refine skills based on execution results (non-blocking)
   576→        if success and self.skill_refiner:
   577→            try:
   578→                # Create execution result dict for refiner
   579→                exec_result = {
   580→                    "success": success,
   581→                    "output": output,
   582→                    "metrics": metrics,
   583→                    "duration_seconds": duration_seconds,
   584→                    "token_metrics": token_metrics,
   585→                }
   586→
   587→                # Call skill refiner
   588→                refined_path = self.skill_refiner.refine(
   589→                    skill_name=skill_name,
   590→                    operation_type=operation_type,
   591→                    execution_result=exec_result,
   592→                    patterns_extracted=decision_paths,
   593→                )
   594→
   595→                if refined_path:
   596→                    logger.info(f"Skill refined: {refined_path}")
   597→                    decision_paths.append(refined_path)
   598→
   599→            except Exception as e:
   600→                logger.debug(
   601→                    "Skill refinement failed (non-blocking): %s", e, exc_info=True
   602→                )
   603→
   604→        # Step 7.5: Check for degradation (non-blocking)
   605→        if self._degradation_detector:
   606→            try:
   607→                degradation_metrics = {
   608→                    "combined_hit_rate": 0.0,
   609→                    "tokens_per_second": 0.0,
   610→                    "mean_coherence": metrics.get("coherence", 0.5),
   611→                    "elapsed_seconds": duration_seconds,
   612→                    "success_rate": 1.0 if success else 0.0,
   613→                }
   614→                if token_metrics:
   615→                    degradation_metrics["combined_hit_rate"] = token_metrics.get(
   616→                        "cache_hit_rate", token_metrics.get("combined_hit_rate", 0.0)
   617→                    )
   618→                    degradation_metrics["tokens_per_second"] = token_metrics.get(
   619→                        "tokens_per_second", 0.0
   620→                    )
   621→                alerts = self._degradation_detector.check_degradation(
   622→                    degradation_metrics
   623→                )
   624→                if alerts:
   625→                    metrics["degradation_alerts"] = len(alerts)
   626→                    for alert in alerts:
   627→                        logger.warning(
   628→                            "Degradation alert [%s]: %s",
   629→                            alert.severity.value,
   630→                            alert.message,
   631→                        )
   632→                    # Log critical alerts to vault
   633→                    critical_alerts = [
   634→                        a
   635→                        for a in alerts
   636→                        if a.severity.value == "CRITICAL"
   637→                    ]
   638→                    for alert in critical_alerts:
   639→                        try:
   640→                            dp = self.log_inflection_point(
   641→                                title=f"Degradation: {alert.metric}",
   642→                                context=f"Task: {task_description}\n{alert.message}",
   643→                                decision="Investigate degradation",
   644→                                rationale=f"Current: {alert.current_value:.3f}, "
   645→                                f"Baseline: {alert.baseline_value:.3f}, "
   646→                                f"Threshold: {alert.threshold:.3f}",
   647→                                project=project,
   648→                            )
   649→                            if dp:
   650→                                decision_paths.append(dp)
   651→                        except Exception as e:
   652→                            logger.debug(
   653→                                "Failed to log degradation alert (non-blocking): %s",
   654→                                e,
   655→                            )
   656→            except Exception as e:
   657→                logger.debug(
   658→                    "Degradation detection failed (non-blocking): %s", e
   659→                )
   660→
   661→        # Step 7.7: Record model quality (non-blocking)
   662→        if self._model_quality_classifier:
   663→            try:
   664→                model_name = "unknown"
   665→                tokens_used_for_quality = 0
   666→                if token_metrics:
   667→                    model_name = token_metrics.get("model", "unknown")
   668→                    tokens_used_for_quality = token_metrics.get("tokens_used", 0)
   669→                self._model_quality_classifier.add_execution(
   670→                    model=model_name,
   671→                    coherence=metrics.get("coherence", 0.5),
   672→                    success=success,
   673→                    tokens_used=tokens_used_for_quality,
   674→                    duration=duration_seconds,
   675→                )
   676→            except Exception as e:
   677→                logger.debug(
   678→                    "Model quality recording failed (non-blocking): %s", e
   679→                )
   680→
   681→        # Step 8: Record metrics (non-blocking)
   682→        if self._metrics_collector:
   683→            try:
   684→                tokens_used = 0
   685→                model_used = ""
   686→                if token_metrics:
   687→                    tokens_used = token_metrics.get("tokens_used", 0)
   688→                    model_used = token_metrics.get("model", "")
   689→                self._metrics_collector.record_execution(
   690→                    skill_name=skill_name,
   691→                    success=success,
   692→                    tokens_used=tokens_used,
   693→                    duration_ms=duration_seconds * 1000,
   694→                    model_used=model_used,
   695→                )
   696→            except Exception as e:
   697→                logger.debug("Metrics recording failed (non-blocking): %s", e)
   698→
   699→        # Step 9: Track journey (non-blocking)
   700→        if self._journey_tracker:
   701→            try:
   702→                temp_result = ExecutionResult(
   703→                    success=success,
   704→                    output=output,
   705→                    metrics=metrics,
   706→                    duration_seconds=duration_seconds,
   707→                    token_metrics=token_metrics,
   708→                )
   709→                point = self._journey_tracker.track_execution(
   710→                    temp_result, task_description, operation_type
   711→                )
   712→                if self._journey_persistence and point:
   713→                    try:
   714→                        point_data = {
   715→                            "coherence": point.coherence,
   716→                            "efficiency": point.efficiency,
   717→                            "operation_type": point.operation_type,
   718→                            "task_description": point.task_description[:200],
   719→                            "timestamp": point.timestamp,
   720→                        }
   721→                        if point.metadata:
   722→                            point_data["metadata"] = point.metadata
   723→                        import asyncio
   724→                        exec_id = f"exec_{int(time.time())}"
   725→                        try:
   726→                            asyncio.get_running_loop()
   727→                            _task = asyncio.ensure_future(  # noqa: RUF006
   728→                                self._journey_persistence
   729→                                .save_trajectory_point(
   730→                                    exec_id, point_data,
   731→                                )
   732→                            )
   733→                        except RuntimeError:
   734→                            asyncio.run(
   735→                                self._journey_persistence
   736→                                .save_trajectory_point(
   737→                                    exec_id, point_data,
   738→                                )
   739→                            )
   740→                    except Exception as e:
   741→                        logger.debug("Journey persistence failed (non-blocking): %s", e)
   742→            except Exception as e:
   743→                logger.debug("Journey tracking failed (non-blocking): %s", e)
   744→
   745→        return ExecutionResult(
   746→            success=success,
   747→            output=output,
   748→            metrics=metrics,
   749→            duration_seconds=duration_seconds,
   750→            vault_experiment_path=experiment_path,
   751→            vault_decision_paths=decision_paths,
   752→            token_metrics=token_metrics,
   753→        )
   754→
   755→    def _compute_token_delta(
   756→        self,
   757→        metrics_before: dict[str, Any] | None,
   758→        metrics_after: dict[str, Any],
   759→    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
   760→        """Compute token metric deltas (tokens used in this execution).
   761→
   762→        Args:
   763→            metrics_before: Token metrics before execution
   764→            metrics_after: Token metrics after execution
   765→
   766→        Returns:
   767→            Dict with token delta information
   768→        """
   769→        if not metrics_before:
   770→            # First execution, can't compute delta
   771→            return metrics_after
   772→
   773→        delta: dict[str, Any] = {}
   774→
   775→        # Compute differences
   776→        if "total_tokens" in metrics_after and "total_tokens" in metrics_before:
   777→            delta["tokens_used"] = (
   778→                metrics_after["total_tokens"] - metrics_before["total_tokens"]
   779→            )
   780→        if "api_calls" in metrics_after and "api_calls" in metrics_before:
   781→            delta["api_calls_made"] = (
   782→                metrics_after["api_calls"] - metrics_before["api_calls"]
   783→            )
   784→        if "cache_hits" in metrics_after and "cache_hits" in metrics_before:
   785→            delta["cache_hits"] = metrics_after["cache_hits"] - metrics_before["cache_hits"]
   786→        if "cache_misses" in metrics_after and "cache_misses" in metrics_before:
   787→            delta["cache_misses"] = (
   788→                metrics_after["cache_misses"] - metrics_before["cache_misses"]
   789→            )
   790→
   791→        # Include final hit rate
   792→        if "cache_hit_rate" in metrics_after:
   793→            delta["cache_hit_rate"] = metrics_after["cache_hit_rate"]
   794→
   795→        # Pass through model name and other non-delta fields
   796→        if "model" in metrics_after:
   797→            delta["model"] = metrics_after["model"]
   798→        if "combined_hit_rate" in metrics_after:
   799→            delta["combined_hit_rate"] = metrics_after["combined_hit_rate"]
   800→        if "tokens_per_second" in metrics_after:
   801→            delta["tokens_per_second"] = metrics_after["tokens_per_second"]
   802→
   803→        return delta
   804→
   805→    def log_inflection_point(
   806→        self,
   807→        title: str,
   808→        context: str,
   809→        decision: str,
   810→        rationale: str,
   811→        project: str = "cohezion",
   812→    ) -> str:
   813→        """Log a critical decision point (called by InflectionDetector).
   814→
   815→        Args:
   816→            title: Decision title
   817→            context: What led to this decision
   818→            decision: The decision made
   819→            rationale: Why this decision was made
   820→            project: Project name
   821→
   822→        Returns:
   823→            Path to vault decision file
   824→        """
   825→        logger.info("Logging inflection point: %s", title)
   826→        result: str = self.logger.log_decision_point(
   827→            project=project,
   828→            title=title,
   829→            context=context,
   830→            decision=decision,
   831→            rationale=rationale,
   832→        )
   833→        return result
   834→
   835→
   836→class ExecutorFactory:
   837→    """Factory for creating compound executors with vault integration."""
   838→
   839→    _instance: CompoundExecutor | None = None
   840→
   841→    @staticmethod

    73→class JourneyTracker:

   218→    def _text_to_latent(self, text: str) -> np.ndarray:
   219→        """Generate deterministic 2048D embedding from text.
   220→
   221→        Uses SHA-256 hash expanded to 2048D with sine wave modulation.
   222→
   223→        Args:
   224→            text: Input text to embed
   225→
   226→        Returns:
   227→            2048D numpy array with normalized values
   228→        """
   229→        # Generate hash
   230→        hash_obj = hashlib.sha256(text.encode())
   231→        hash_bytes = hash_obj.digest()
   232→
   233→        # Expand to 2048 dimensions using deterministic method
   234→        latent = np.zeros(self.HASH_DIMS)
   235→        for i in range(self.HASH_DIMS):
   236→            # Cycle through hash bytes
   237→            byte_idx = i % len(hash_bytes)
   238→            # Use sine wave modulation for smooth variation
   239→            phase = (2.0 * np.pi * i) / self.HASH_DIMS
   240→            latent[i] = (
   241→                (hash_bytes[byte_idx] / 255.0) * 0.5
   242→                + 0.25 * np.sin(phase)
   243→                + 0.25 * np.cos(phase * 2)
   244→            )
   245→
   246→        # Normalize to [-1, 1]
   247→        latent = 2.0 * (latent - np.min(latent)) / (np.max(latent) - np.min(latent) + 1e-8) - 1.0
   248→
   249→        return latent
   250→
   251→    def _holographic_project(self, latent_2048d: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
   252→        """Project 2048D embedding to 12D using holographic method.
   253→
   254→        Uses chunk-mean averaging: divide 2048D into 128-element segments
   255→        and take mean of each segment. Then interpolate to 12D.
   256→
   257→        Args:
   258→            latent_2048d: 2048D embedding vector
   259→
   260→        Returns:
   261→            12D normalized vector
   262→        """
   263→        # Check cache
   264→        latent_hash = hashlib.sha256(latent_2048d.tobytes()).hexdigest()[:8]
   265→        if latent_hash in self._projection_cache:
   266→            return self._projection_cache[latent_hash]
   267→
   268→        # Chunk-mean projection: 2048 → 16 dimensions (128-element chunks)
   269→        num_chunks = self.HASH_DIMS // self.CHUNK_SIZE
   270→        chunk_means = np.array([
   271→            np.mean(latent_2048d[i * self.CHUNK_SIZE:(i + 1) * self.CHUNK_SIZE])
   272→            for i in range(num_chunks)
   273→        ])
   274→
   275→        # Interpolate 16D → 12D
   276→        if len(chunk_means) > self.AXIOMATIC_DIMS:
   277→            # Downsample using linear interpolation
   278→            indices = np.linspace(0, len(chunk_means) - 1, self.AXIOMATIC_DIMS)
   279→            result_12d = np.interp(indices, np.arange(len(chunk_means)), chunk_means)
   280→        else:
   281→            # Upsample if needed
   282→            indices = np.linspace(0, len(chunk_means) - 1, self.AXIOMATIC_DIMS)
   283→            result_12d = np.interp(indices, np.arange(len(chunk_means)), chunk_means)
   284→
   285→        # Normalize to [0, 1]
   286→        result_12d = (result_12d - np.min(result_12d)) / (
   287→            np.max(result_12d) - np.min(result_12d) + 1e-8
   288→        )
   289→
   290→        # Cache result
   291→        self._projection_cache[latent_hash] = result_12d
   292→
   293→        return result_12d
   294→
   295→    def _step_to_axiomatic(
   296→        self,
   297→        projection_12d: np.ndarray,
   298→        operation_type: str,
   299→        coherence: float,
   300→        efficiency: float,
   301→    ) -> np.ndarray:
   302→        """Apply operation-specific modulation to 12D projection.
   303→
   304→        Combines base projection with operation modulation and
   305→        execution quality metrics.
   306→
   307→        Args:
   308→            projection_12d: 12D base projection
   309→            operation_type: Type of operation
   310→            coherence: Quality metric (0.0-1.0)
   311→            efficiency: Token efficiency (0.0-1.0)
   312→
   313→        Returns:
   314→            12D axiomatic vector
   315→        """
   316→        # Get modulation profile
   317→        operation_str = (
   318→            operation_type
   319→            if isinstance(operation_type, str)
   320→            else operation_type.value
   321→        )
   322→        modulation = self._modulation_profiles.get(
   323→            operation_str,
   324→            self._modulation_profiles[OperationType.TRANSFORM.value],
   325→        )
   326→
   327→        # Combine projection with modulation
   328→        # Weight modulation by execution quality
   329→        quality_weight = 0.5 * coherence + 0.5 * efficiency
   330→        axiomatic = (
   331→            projection_12d * (1.0 - quality_weight) + modulation * quality_weight
   332→        )
   333→
   334→        # Normalize
   335→        axiomatic = np.clip(axiomatic, 0.0, 1.0)
   336→
   337→        return axiomatic
   338→
   339→    def _compute_phi_score(
   340→        self,
   341→        coherence: float,
   342→        smoothness: float = 0.5,
   343→        convergence: float = 0.5,
   344→    ) -> float:
   345→        """Compute trajectory quality score (phi score).
   346→
   347→        Combines coherence (execution quality) with trajectory smoothness
   348→        and convergence (stability indicators).
   349→
   350→        Args:
   351→            coherence: Execution coherence metric
   352→            smoothness: Trajectory smoothness (0.0-1.0)
   353→            convergence: Convergence to stable state (0.0-1.0)
   354→
   355→        Returns:
   356→            Phi score (0.0-1.0)
   357→        """
   358→        phi = (
   359→            coherence * 0.5 + smoothness * 0.3 + convergence * 0.2
   360→        )
   361→        return np.clip(phi, 0.0, 1.0)
   362→
   363→    def track_execution(
   364→        self,
   365→        execution_result: ExecutionResult,
   366→        task_description: str,
   367→        operation_type: str,
   368→    ) -> TrajectoryPoint:
   369→        """Track a compound execution as a trajectory point.
   370→
   371→        Args:
   372→            execution_result: ExecutionResult from compound executor
   373→            task_description: Description of the task
   374→            operation_type: Type of operation
   375→
   376→        Returns:
   377→            TrajectoryPoint with 12D trajectory coordinates
   378→        """
   379→        # Extract metrics
   380→        coherence = execution_result.metrics.get("coherence", 0.5)
   381→        efficiency = (
   382→            execution_result.token_metrics.get("cache_hit_rate", 0.5)
   383→            if execution_result.token_metrics
   384→            else 0.5
   385→        )
   386→
   387→        # Generate embeddings
   388→        latent_2048d = self._text_to_latent(task_description)
   389→        projection_12d = self._holographic_project(latent_2048d)
   390→
   391→        # Apply operation modulation
   392→        axiomatic_12d = self._step_to_axiomatic(
   393→            projection_12d, operation_type, coherence, efficiency
   394→        )
   395→
   396→        # Compute quality score
   397→        phi_score = self._compute_phi_score(
   398→            coherence=coherence,
   399→            smoothness=0.5,  # TODO: compute from trajectory history
   400→            convergence=0.5,  # TODO: compute from trajectory history
   401→        )
   402→
   403→        point = TrajectoryPoint(
   404→            dimensions=axiomatic_12d,
   405→            timestamp=execution_result.duration_seconds,
   406→            coherence=coherence,
   407→            efficiency=efficiency,
   408→            operation_type=operation_type,
   409→            task_description=task_description,
   410→            metadata={
   411→                "phi_score": phi_score,
   412→                "success": execution_result.success,
   413→                "output_length": len(execution_result.output),
   414→            },
   415→        )
   416→
   417→        logger.debug(
   418→            "Tracked execution: %s (phi=%.2f, coherence=%.2f)",
   419→            task_description[:50],
   420→            phi_score,
   421→            coherence,
   422→        )
   423→
   424→        return point
   425→
   426→    def compute_trajectory_quality(
   427→        self,
   428→        points: list[TrajectoryPoint],
   429→    ) -> dict[str, float]:
   430→        """Compute comprehensive trajectory quality metrics.
   431→
   432→        Args:
   433→            points: List of trajectory points
   434→
   435→        Returns:
   436→            Dictionary with quality metrics
   437→        """
   438→        if not points:
   439→            return {
   440→                "mean_phi_score": 0.0,
   441→                "mean_coherence": 0.0,
   442→                "mean_efficiency": 0.0,
   443→                "smoothness": 0.0,
   444→                "convergence": 0.0,
   445→            }
   446→
   447→        coherences = np.array([p.coherence for p in points])
   448→        efficiencies = np.array([p.efficiency for p in points])
   449→        phi_scores = np.array([p.metadata["phi_score"] for p in points])
   450→
   451→        # Compute smoothness (variance of dimension changes)
   452→        dimensions = np.array([p.dimensions for p in points])
   453→        if len(dimensions) > 1:
   454→            diffs = np.diff(dimensions, axis=0)
   455→            smoothness = 1.0 - np.mean(np.abs(diffs))
   456→        else:
   457→            smoothness = 1.0
   458→
   459→        # Compute convergence (trend toward stable state)
   460→        if len(coherences) > 1:
   461→            convergence = 1.0 - np.std(coherences[-min(3, len(coherences)):])
   462→        else:
   463→            convergence = 1.0
   464→
   465→        return {
   466→            "mean_phi_score": float(np.mean(phi_scores)),
   467→            "mean_coherence": float(np.mean(coherences)),
   468→            "mean_efficiency": float(np.mean(efficiencies)),
   469→            "smoothness": float(np.clip(smoothness, 0.0, 1.0)),
   470→            "convergence": float(np.clip(convergence, 0.0, 1.0)),
   471→        }
   472→
   473→
   474→class JourneyTrackerFactory:
   475→    """Factory for creating journey trackers."""
   476→
   477→    @staticmethod
   478→    def create(seed: int = 42) -> JourneyTracker:
   479→        """Create a journey tracker.
   480→
   481→        Args:
   482→            seed: Random seed for deterministic behavior
   483→
   484→        Returns:
   485→            JourneyTracker instance
   486→        """
   487→        return JourneyTracker(seed=seed)
   488→
```

     1→"""Collect execution experiences from Parquet, SurrealDB, and vault sources.
     2→
     3→Three tiers, each non-blocking:
     4→  Tier 1: Parquet journey shards  (data/journeys/shard_*.parquet)
     5→  Tier 2: SurrealDB mission_journey table
     6→  Tier 3: Vault experiment JSON files
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→import json
    12→import logging
    13→from pathlib import Path
    14→
    15→import numpy as np
    16→
    17→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    18→
    19→# Default paths
    20→_PARQUET_DIR = Path("data/journeys")
    21→_VAULT_DIR = Path.home() / "vaults" / "cohezion-vault" / "experiments"
    22→
    23→
    24→class ExperienceCollector:
    25→    """Gather execution experiences from all available data sources.
    26→
    27→    Parameters
    28→    ----------
    29→    parquet_dir : Path
    30→        Directory containing ``shard_*.parquet`` files.
    31→    vault_dir : Path
    32→        Directory containing vault experiment JSON files.
    33→    """
    34→
    35→    def __init__(
    36→        self,
    37→        parquet_dir: Path | str = _PARQUET_DIR,
    38→        vault_dir: Path | str = _VAULT_DIR,
    39→    ) -> None:
    40→        self.parquet_dir = Path(parquet_dir)
    41→        self.vault_dir = Path(vault_dir)
    42→
    43→    def collect_all(self, max_samples: int = 100_000) -> list[dict]:
    44→        """Collect experiences from all tiers.
    45→
    46→        Returns a list of normalized experience dicts with keys matching
    47→        the ExperienceEncoder schema.
    48→        """
    49→        records: list[dict] = []
    50→        records.extend(self._collect_parquet(max_samples))
    51→        if len(records) < max_samples:
    52→            records.extend(self._collect_surreal(max_samples - len(records)))
    53→        if len(records) < max_samples:
    54→            records.extend(self._collect_vault(max_samples - len(records)))
    55→        logger.info("Collected %d total experience records", len(records))
    56→        return records[:max_samples]
    57→
    58→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    59→    # Tier 1: Parquet shards
    60→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    61→    def _collect_parquet(self, max_samples: int) -> list[dict]:
    62→        """Read journey shards from Parquet files."""
    63→        if not self.parquet_dir.is_dir():
    64→            logger.debug("Parquet dir %s not found, skipping tier 1", self.parquet_dir)
    65→            return []
    66→        parquet_files = sorted(self.parquet_dir.glob("shard_*.parquet"))
    67→        if not parquet_files:
    68→            return []
    69→
    70→        try:
    71→            import pyarrow.parquet as pq
    72→        except ImportError:
    73→            logger.debug("pyarrow not installed, skipping parquet tier")
    74→            return []
    75→
    76→        records: list[dict] = []
    77→        for pf in parquet_files:
    78→            if len(records) >= max_samples:
    79→                break
    80→            try:
    81→                table = pq.read_table(pf)
    82→                for row in table.to_pydict().values():
    83→                    # pydict returns {col: [values]} — need to iterate rows
    84→                    break  # just need to check structure
    85→                df_rows = table.to_pandas().to_dict(orient="records")
    86→                for row in df_rows:
    87→                    if len(records) >= max_samples:
    88→                        break
    89→                    records.append(self._normalize_parquet_row(row))
    90→            except Exception as e:
    91→                logger.debug("Skipping %s: %s", pf, e)
    92→        logger.info("Tier 1 (parquet): collected %d records", len(records))
    93→        return records
    94→
    95→    @staticmethod
    96→    def _normalize_parquet_row(row: dict) -> dict:
    97→        """Convert a parquet row into the canonical experience schema."""
    98→        # Extract the last 12D trajectory point (or average if multiple)
    99→        trajectory = np.zeros(12, dtype=np.float32)
   100→        state_traj = row.get("state_trajectory")
   101→        if state_traj is not None and len(state_traj) > 0:
   102→            # state_trajectory is list of 12D arrays — take last point
   103→            last = state_traj[-1]
   104→            arr = np.asarray(last, dtype=np.float32).ravel()
   105→            n = min(len(arr), 12)
   106→            trajectory[:n] = arr[:n]
   107→
   108→        return {
   109→            "trajectory": trajectory,
   110→            "mission_id": row.get("mission_id", ""),
   111→            "agent_id": row.get("agent_id", ""),
   112→            "skill_name": row.get("skill_name", ""),
   113→            "input_preview": row.get("input_preview", ""),
   114→            "phi_score": float(row.get("phi_score", 0.0)),
   115→            "novelty": float(row.get("novelty", 0.0)),
   116→            "operation_type": "generate",  # default for journeys
   117→        }
   118→
   119→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   120→    # Tier 2: SurrealDB mission_journey
   121→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   122→    def _collect_surreal(self, max_samples: int) -> list[dict]:
   123→        """Query SurrealDB for mission journey records (non-blocking)."""
   124→        try:
   125→            import asyncio
   126→
   127→            try:
   128→                loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
   129→            except RuntimeError:
   130→                loop = None
   131→
   132→            if loop and loop.is_running():
   133→                # Already inside an async context — can't nest run_until_complete.
   134→                # Skip SurrealDB tier; it'll be tried next session.
   135→                logger.debug("SurrealDB tier skipped: event loop already running")
   136→                return []
   137→
   138→            return asyncio.run(self._collect_surreal_async(max_samples))
   139→        except Exception as e:
   140→            logger.debug("SurrealDB tier skipped: %s", e)
   141→            return []
   142→
   143→    async def _collect_surreal_async(self, max_samples: int) -> list[dict]:
   144→        """Async SurrealDB collection."""
   145→        try:
   146→            from cohezion.core.persistence.surreal_client import SurrealClient
   147→
   148→            client = SurrealClient()
   149→            await client.connect()
   150→            result = await client.query(
   151→                f"SELECT * FROM mission_journey LIMIT {max_samples}"
   152→            )
   153→            records = []
   154→            if result and isinstance(result, list):
   155→                for row in result:
   156→                    if isinstance(row, dict):
   157→                        records.append(self._normalize_surreal_row(row))
   158→            logger.info("Tier 2 (surreal): collected %d records", len(records))
   159→            return records
   160→        except Exception as e:
   161→            logger.debug("SurrealDB query failed: %s", e)
   162→            return []
   163→
   164→    @staticmethod
   165→    def _normalize_surreal_row(row: dict) -> dict:
   166→        """Normalize a SurrealDB mission_journey record."""
   167→        trajectory = np.zeros(12, dtype=np.float32)
   168→        state_traj = row.get("state_trajectory")
   169→        if state_traj is not None and len(state_traj) > 0:
   170→            last = state_traj[-1] if isinstance(state_traj[-1], (list, np.ndarray)) else state_traj
   171→            arr = np.asarray(last, dtype=np.float32).ravel()
   172→            n = min(len(arr), 12)
   173→            trajectory[:n] = arr[:n]
   174→
   175→        return {
   176→            "trajectory": trajectory,
   177→            "mission_id": row.get("id", row.get("mission_id", "")),
   178→            "agent_id": row.get("agent_id", ""),
   179→            "skill_name": row.get("skill_name", ""),
   180→            "input_preview": "",
   181→            "phi_score": float(row.get("phi_score", 0.0)),
   182→            "operation_type": row.get("operation_type", "generate"),
   183→        }
   184→
   185→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   186→    # Tier 3: Vault experiment JSONs
   187→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   188→    def _collect_vault(self, max_samples: int) -> list[dict]:
   189→        """Walk vault experiment files for experience data."""
   190→        if not self.vault_dir.is_dir():
   191→            logger.debug("Vault dir %s not found, skipping tier 3", self.vault_dir)
   192→            return []
   193→
   194→        records: list[dict] = []
   195→        for json_path in sorted(self.vault_dir.rglob("*.json")):
   196→            if len(records) >= max_samples:
   197→                break
   198→            try:
   199→                with open(json_path) as f:
   200→                    data = json.load(f)
   201→                if isinstance(data, dict):
   202→                    records.append(self._normalize_vault_record(data, json_path.stem))
   203→                elif isinstance(data, list):
   204→                    for item in data:
   205→                        if isinstance(item, dict) and len(records) < max_samples:
   206→                            records.append(self._normalize_vault_record(item, json_path.stem))
   207→            except Exception as e:
   208→                logger.debug("Skipping vault file %s: %s", json_path, e)
   209→
   210→        logger.info("Tier 3 (vault): collected %d records", len(records))
   211→        return records
   212→
   213→    @staticmethod
   214→    def _normalize_vault_record(record: dict, source: str) -> dict:
   215→        """Normalize a vault experiment JSON into the canonical schema."""
   216→        return {
   217→            "trajectory": np.zeros(12, dtype=np.float32),
   218→            "mission_id": record.get("id", source),
   219→            "agent_id": record.get("agent_id", ""),
   220→            "skill_name": record.get("skill_name", record.get("title", "")),
   221→            "input_preview": record.get("hypothesis", record.get("description", "")),
   222→            "phi_score": float(record.get("phi_score", 0.0)),
   223→            "operation_type": record.get("operation_type", "analyze"),
   224→        }
   225→

```
     1→"""Encode agentic execution experiences as 256D vectors for FLUME VAE training.
     2→
     3→256D encoding scheme:
     4→  [0:12]   12D axiomatic trajectory (from JourneyTracker)
     5→  [12:24]  12 scalar execution metrics
     6→  [24:29]  5 operation type one-hot
     7→  [29:256] 227 semantic fingerprint (deterministic SHA-256 hash expansion)
     8→"""
     9→
    10→from __future__ import annotations
    11→
    12→import hashlib
    13→import math
    14→
    15→import numpy as np
    16→
    17→# Dimension layout
    18→_TRAJECTORY_DIM = 12  # dims [0:12]
    19→_METRICS_DIM = 12  # dims [12:24]
    20→_OP_TYPE_DIM = 5  # dims [24:29]
    21→_FINGERPRINT_DIM = 227  # dims [29:256]
    22→TOTAL_DIM = _TRAJECTORY_DIM + _METRICS_DIM + _OP_TYPE_DIM + _FINGERPRINT_DIM  # 256
    23→
    24→# Canonical operation types (matches JourneyTracker.OperationType)
    25→OPERATION_TYPES = ("generate", "analyze", "search", "transform", "persist")
    26→
    27→# Metric keys in canonical order (dims 12-23)
    28→METRIC_KEYS = (
    29→    "phi_score",
    30→    "anomaly_score",
    31→    "misalignment_score",
    32→    "intent_confidence",
    33→    "duration_s",
    34→    "tokens_used",
    35→    "cache_hit_rate",
    36→    "success",
    37→    "token_efficiency",
    38→    "quality_score",
    39→    "convergence_trend",
    40→    "cost_usd",
    41→)
    42→
    43→
    44→class ExperienceEncoder:
    45→    """Encode a single execution experience dict into a 256D float32 vector.
    46→
    47→    The encoding is fully deterministic: same input dict always produces
    48→    the same output vector.
    49→    """
    50→
    51→    def encode(self, experience: dict) -> np.ndarray:
    52→        """Encode an experience record into a 256D float32 vector.
    53→
    54→        Parameters
    55→        ----------
    56→        experience : dict
    57→            Must contain at least one of:
    58→            - ``trajectory``: list/array of 12D floats
    59→            - ``operation_type``: one of OPERATION_TYPES
    60→            Additional scalar metrics are pulled by METRIC_KEYS names.
    61→
    62→        Returns
    63→        -------
    64→        np.ndarray
    65→            Shape ``(256,)`` dtype ``float32``.
    66→        """
    67→        vec = np.zeros(TOTAL_DIM, dtype=np.float32)
    68→
    69→        # --- Dims [0:12]: 12D trajectory ---
    70→        traj = experience.get("trajectory")
    71→        if traj is not None:
    72→            traj_arr = np.asarray(traj, dtype=np.float32).ravel()
    73→            n = min(len(traj_arr), _TRAJECTORY_DIM)
    74→            vec[:n] = traj_arr[:n]
    75→
    76→        # --- Dims [12:24]: scalar execution metrics ---
    77→        for i, key in enumerate(METRIC_KEYS):
    78→            raw = experience.get(key, 0.0)
    79→            val = float(raw) if raw is not None else 0.0
    80→            # Log-scale normalization for duration and tokens
    81→            if key == "duration_s":
    82→                val = math.log1p(val) / 10.0  # log(1+s)/10 → ~0-1 for 0-22000s
    83→            elif key == "tokens_used":
    84→                val = math.log1p(val) / 15.0  # log(1+t)/15 → ~0-1 for 0-3M tokens
    85→            elif key == "cost_usd":
    86→                val = math.log1p(val * 100) / 10.0  # scale cents then log
    87→            # Clamp to [0,1] for bounded metrics
    88→            vec[_TRAJECTORY_DIM + i] = float(np.clip(val, -1.0, 2.0))
    89→
    90→        # --- Dims [24:29]: operation type one-hot ---
    91→        op_type = experience.get("operation_type", "")
    92→        if isinstance(op_type, str):
    93→            op_type = op_type.lower()
    94→        if op_type in OPERATION_TYPES:
    95→            idx = OPERATION_TYPES.index(op_type)
    96→            vec[_TRAJECTORY_DIM + _METRICS_DIM + idx] = 1.0
    97→
    98→        # --- Dims [29:256]: semantic fingerprint ---
    99→        fingerprint_text = self._build_fingerprint_text(experience)
   100→        vec[_TRAJECTORY_DIM + _METRICS_DIM + _OP_TYPE_DIM :] = (
   101→            self._sha256_expand(fingerprint_text, _FINGERPRINT_DIM)
   102→        )
   103→
   104→        return vec
   105→
   106→    @staticmethod
   107→    def _build_fingerprint_text(experience: dict) -> str:
   108→        """Build a stable text key from experience metadata for hashing."""
   109→        parts = []
   110→        for key in ("mission_id", "agent_id", "skill_name", "input_preview"):
   111→            val = experience.get(key)
   112→            if val:
   113→                parts.append(str(val))
   114→        return "|".join(parts) if parts else "unknown"
   115→
   116→    @staticmethod
   117→    def _sha256_expand(text: str, dim: int) -> np.ndarray:
   118→        """Deterministic hash expansion to ``dim`` floats in [0, 1].
   119→
   120→        Same approach as JourneyTracker._text_to_latent: SHA-256 bytes
   121→        cycled with sine-wave modulation for smooth variation.
   122→        """
   123→        hash_bytes = hashlib.sha256(text.encode("utf-8")).digest()
   124→        out = np.zeros(dim, dtype=np.float32)
   125→        for i in range(dim):
   126→            byte_val = hash_bytes[i % len(hash_bytes)]
   127→            phase = (2.0 * math.pi * i) / dim
   128→            out[i] = (
   129→                (byte_val / 255.0) * 0.5
   130→                + 0.25 * math.sin(phase)
   131→                + 0.25 * math.cos(phase * 2)
   132→            )
   133→        return out
   134→
```
