- ❌ VAE latent space (Phase 5) is trained but never queried
- ❌ No connection between past trajectories and current execution

**Maximum Compound Opportunity: Phase 9 - Experience-Guided Execution Loop**

This closes the exponential learning loop:

```
Execute task → Track trajectory (Phases 1-4) →
Store in ExperienceCollector (Phase 2) →
Train VAE on trajectories (Phase 5) →
Query VAE for similar tasks (NEW) →
Guide next execution with trajectory insights (NEW) →
Better trajectory → Loop again (exponential improvement)
```

### Why This Creates Maximum Compound Value

1. **Leverages ALL 7 phases we just built**:
   - Phase 1: Real cohesion scores → Know what "good" looks like
   - Phase 2: Trajectory statistics → Searchable similarity
   - Phase 4: Phi_score → Quality metric for recommendations
   - Phase 5: VAE trained on real data → Can find similar trajectories
   - Phase 6: Retrospection → Filters noise from learning
   - Phase 7: Universe bridge → Tracks where in manifold success happens

2. **Exponential multiplier**: Every execution makes EVERY future execution better

3. **Already 80% built**: Infrastructure exists, just needs connection

4. **Unlocks the VAE**: Currently trained but unused for guidance

Return top-k trajectories with outcome data\n\nExample:\n    ```python\n    search = TrajectorySearchEngine(collector, encoder)\n\n    results = search.find_similar_trajectories(\n        task_description=\"Generate creative ideas\",\n        operation_type=\"generate\",\n        top_k=5,\n    )\n\n    for result in results:\n        print(f\"Task: {result.task}, coherence: {result.coherence:.2f}\")\n        print(f\"Recommendation: {result.guidance}\")\n    ```\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom typing import Any\n\nimport numpy as np\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass TrajectorySearchResult:\n    \"\"\"Single search result from trajectory database.\"\"\"\n\n    task_description: str\n    operation_type: str\n    coherence: float\n    phi_score: float\n    trajectory_smoothness: float\n    trajectory_convergence: float\n    similarity_score: float  # Cosine similarity to query\n    success: bool\n    guidance: str  # Human-readable recommendation\n\n\nclass TrajectorySearchEngine:\n    \"\"\"Search engine for finding similar past execution trajectories.\n\n    Uses semantic similarity (via ExperienceEncoder) to find tasks similar\n    to the current one, then ranks by execution quality metrics to provide\n    actionable guidance.\n\n    Example:\n        ```python\n        from cohezion.flume.experience_collector import ExperienceCollector\n        from cohezion.flume.experience_encoder import ExperienceEncoder\n\n        collector = ExperienceCollector()\n        encoder = ExperienceEncoder()\n        search = TrajectorySearchEngine(collector, encoder)\n\n        results = search.find_similar_trajectories(\n            \"Implement new feature\",\n            \"generate\",\n            top_k=3,\n        )\n        ```\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        collector: Any,  # ExperienceCollector\n        encoder: Any,  # ExperienceEncoder\n        similarity_threshold: float = 0.5,\n    ):\n        \"\"\"Initialize trajectory search engine.\n\n        Args:\n            collector: ExperienceCollector instance\n            encoder: ExperienceEncoder instance\n            similarity_threshold: Minimum cosine similarity for matches\n        \"\"\"\n        self.collector = collector\n        self.encoder = encoder\n        self.similarity_threshold = similarity_threshold\n        logger.debug(\"Initialized TrajectorySearchEngine (threshold=%.2f)\", similarity_threshold)\n\n    def find_similar_trajectories(\n        self,\n        task_description: str,\n        operation_type: str,\n        top_k: int = 5,\n        min_coherence: float = 0.4,  # HIHO threshold\n    ) -> list[TrajectorySearchResult]:\n        \"\"\"Find past trajectories similar to current task.\n\n        Args:\n            task_description: Description of current task\n            operation_type: Type of operation (generate, analyze, etc.)\n            top_k: Number of results to return\n            min_coherence: Minimum coherence for recommendations\n\n        Returns:\n            List of TrajectorySearchResult, ranked by similarity * quality\n        \"\"\"\n        # Step 1: Get all experiences from collector\n        try:\n            experiences = self.collector.collect_all(max_samples=1000)\n        except Exception as e:\n            logger.warning(\"Failed to collect experiences: %s\", e)\n            return []\n\n        if not experiences:\n            logger.debug(\"No experiences found for trajectory search\")\n            return []\n\n        # Step 2: Encode query task\n        query_vector = self._encode_task(task_description, operation_type)\n\n        # Step 3: Compute similarity to all experiences\n        results = []\n        for exp in experiences:\n            # Encode experience\n            exp_vector = self.encoder.encode_experience(exp)\n\n            # Compute cosine similarity\n            similarity = self._cosine_similarity(query_vector, exp_vector)\n\n            if similarity < self.similarity_threshold:\n                continue  # Skip low-similarity matches\n\n            # Extract metrics\n            coherence = exp.get(\"coherence\", 0.5)\n            phi_score = exp.get(\"phi_score\", 0.5)\n            smoothness = exp.get(\"trajectory_smoothness\", 0.5)\n            convergence = exp.get(\"trajectory_convergence\", 0.5)\n            success = exp.get(\"success\", False)\n\n            # Filter by minimum coherence (don't learn from bad executions)\n            if coherence < min_coherence:\n                continue\n\n            # Generate guidance\n            guidance = self._generate_guidance(\n                exp, coherence, phi_score, smoothness, convergence, success\n            )\n\n            results.append(\n                TrajectorySearchResult(\n                    task_description=exp.get(\"task_description\", \"\"),\n                    operation_type=exp.get(\"operation_type\", \"\"),\n                    coherence=coherence,\n                    phi_score=phi_score,\n                    trajectory_smoothness=smoothness,\n                    trajectory_convergence=convergence,\n                    similarity_score=similarity,\n                    success=success,\n                    guidance=guidance,\n                )\n            )\n\n        # Step 4: Rank by combined score (similarity * quality)\n        results.sort(\n            key=lambda r: r.similarity_score * (r.coherence * 0.5 + r.phi_score * 0.5),\n            reverse=True,\n        )\n\n        # Step 5: Return top-k\n        top_results = results[:top_k]\n        logger.info(\n            \"Found %d similar trajectories (top coherence: %.2f, similarity: %.2f)\",\n            len(top_results),\n            top_results[0].coherence if top_results else 0.0,\n            top_results[0].similarity_score if top_results else 0.0,\n        )\n\n        return top_results\n\n    def _encode_task(self, task_description: str, operation_type: str) -> np.ndarray:\n        \"\"\"Encode task description using ExperienceEncoder.\n\n        Args:\n            task_description: Task description text\n            operation_type: Operation type\n\n        Returns:\n            256D encoded vector\n        \"\"\"\n        # Create minimal experience dict for encoding\n        exp = {\n            \"task_description\": task_description,\n            \"operation_type\": operation_type,\n            # Default values for required fields\n            \"coherence\": 0.5,\n            \"phi_score\": 0.5,\n            \"trajectory_smoothness\": 0.5,\n            \"trajectory_convergence\": 0.5,\n            \"success\": True,\n            \"state_trajectory\": [],\n        }\n\n        return self.encoder.encode_experience(exp)\n\n    def _cosine_similarity(self, a: np.ndarray, b: np.ndarray) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Compute cosine similarity between two vectors.\n\n        Args:\n            a: First vector\n            b: Second vector\n\n        Returns:\n            Cosine similarity (0.0-1.0)\n        \"\"\"\n        dot_product = np.dot(a, b)\n        norm_a = np.linalg.norm(a)\n        norm_b = np.linalg.norm(b)\n\n        if norm_a == 0 or norm_b == 0:\n            return 0.0\n\n        return float(dot_product / (norm_a * norm_b))\n\n    def _generate_guidance(\n        self,\n        exp: dict,\n        coherence: float,\n        phi_score: float,\n        smoothness: float,\n        convergence: float,\n        success: bool,\n    ) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Generate human-readable guidance from trajectory data.\n\n        Args:\n            exp: Experience dictionary\n            coherence: Coherence score\n            phi_score: Trajectory quality score\n            smoothness: Trajectory smoothness\n            convergence: HIHO convergence\n            success: Whether execution succeeded\n\n        Returns:\n            Guidance text\n        \"\"\"\n        if not success:\n            return f\"Similar task failed (coherence={coherence:.2f}). Approach with caution.\"\n\n        if coherence >= 0.7 and phi_score >= 0.7:\n            return f\"Similar task had excellent outcomes (coherence={coherence:.2f}, phi={phi_score:.2f}). High confidence.\"\n        elif coherence >= 0.5:\n            return f\"Similar task succeeded (coherence={coherence:.2f}). Moderate confidence.\"\n        else:\n            return f\"Similar task barely succeeded (coherence={coherence:.2f}). Low confidence.\"\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/src/cohezion/compound/guidance_enhancer.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Guidance enhancer for experience-guided execution.\n\nTransforms trajectory search results into actionable execution guidance.\nAggregates insights from similar past executions to provide:\n- Recommended approaches from successful trajectories\n- Warnings from failed trajectories\n- Confidence scores based on similarity and outcome quality\n\nArchitecture:\n    1. Receive trajectory search results\n    2. Aggregate patterns across results\n    3. Generate recommendations (do's and don'ts)\n    4. Compute confidence scores\n    5. Return enhanced guidance dict for executor\n\nExample:\n    ```python\n    enhancer = GuidanceEnhancer()\n\n    enhanced = enhancer.enhance_guidance(\n        base_guidance={\"decisions\": [...], \"patterns\": [...]},\n        trajectory_results=[...],  # From TrajectorySearchEngine\n    )\n\n    # enhanced includes:\n    # - recommendations: What worked well\n    # - warnings: What to avoid\n    # - confidence: How reliable this guidance is\n    ```\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom typing import Any\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass EnhancedGuidance:\n    \"\"\"Enhanced execution guidance from trajectory analysis.\"\"\"\n\n    recommendations: list[str]  # What to do (from successful trajectories)\n    warnings: list[str]  # What to avoid (from failed trajectories)\n    confidence: float  # Overall confidence in guidance (0.0-1.0)\n    similar_task_count: int  # Number of similar past tasks found\n    avg_coherence: float  # Average coherence of similar tasks\n    avg_phi_score: float  # Average trajectory quality\n    base_guidance: dict[str, Any]  # Original vault guidance\n\n\nclass GuidanceEnhancer:\n    \"\"\"Enhance execution guidance using trajectory analysis.\n\n    Aggregates insights from similar past trajectories to provide\n    actionable recommendations for current execution.\n\n    Example:\n        ```python\n        from cohezion.compound.trajectory_search import TrajectorySearchEngine\n\n        search = TrajectorySearchEngine(collector, encoder)\n        enhancer = GuidanceEnhancer()\n\n        # Find similar trajectories\n        results = search.find_similar_trajectories(\n            \"Implement new feature\", \"generate\"\n        )\n\n        # Enhance guidance\n        enhanced = enhancer.enhance_guidance(\n            base_guidance={\"decisions\": [], \"patterns\": []},\n            trajectory_results=results,\n        )\n\n        print(f\"Confidence: {enhanced.confidence:.2f}\")\n        for rec in enhanced.recommendations:\n            print(f\"  ✓ {rec}\")\n        ```\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        min_confidence_threshold: float = 0.5,\n        high_quality_threshold: float = 0.7,\n    ):\n        \"\"\"Initialize guidance enhancer.\n\n        Args:\n            min_confidence_threshold: Minimum confidence for recommendations\n            high_quality_threshold: Coherence threshold for \"high quality\"\n        \"\"\"\n        self.min_confidence_threshold = min_confidence_threshold\n        self.high_quality_threshold = high_quality_threshold\n        logger.debug(\n            \"Initialized GuidanceEnhancer (min_confidence=%.2f, high_quality=%.2f)\",\n            min_confidence_threshold,\n            high_quality_threshold,\n        )\n\n    def enhance_guidance(\n        self,\n        base_guidance: dict[str, Any],\n        trajectory_results: list[Any],  # TrajectorySearchResult\n    ) -> EnhancedGuidance:\n        \"\"\"Enhance guidance with trajectory analysis.\n\n        Args:\n            base_guidance: Base guidance from vault (decisions, patterns, etc.)\n            trajectory_results: Search results from TrajectorySearchEngine\n\n        Returns:\n            EnhancedGuidance with recommendations, warnings, confidence\n        \"\"\"\n        if not trajectory_results:\n            logger.debug(\"No trajectory results, returning base guidance only\")\n            return EnhancedGuidance(\n                recommendations=[],\n                warnings=[],\n                confidence=0.0,\n                similar_task_count=0,\n                avg_coherence=0.0,\n                avg_phi_score=0.0,\n                base_guidance=base_guidance,\n            )\n\n        # Separate successful and failed trajectories\n        successful = [r for r in trajectory_results if r.success and r.coherence >= 0.5]\n        failed = [r for r in trajectory_results if not r.success or r.coherence < 0.5]\n\n        # Generate recommendations from successful trajectories\n        recommendations = self._generate_recommendations(successful)\n\n        # Generate warnings from failed trajectories\n        warnings = self._generate_warnings(failed)\n\n        # Compute aggregate metrics\n        all_coherences = [r.coherence for r in trajectory_results]\n        all_phi_scores = [r.phi_score for r in trajectory_results]\n        avg_coherence = sum(all_coherences) / len(all_coherences) if all_coherences else 0.0\n        avg_phi_score = sum(all_phi_scores) / len(all_phi_scores) if all_phi_scores else 0.0\n\n        # Compute confidence (higher if many high-quality results)\n        high_quality_count = sum(\n            1 for r in trajectory_results\n            if r.coherence >= self.high_quality_threshold and r.success\n        )\n        confidence = min(\n            1.0,\n            (high_quality_count / max(1, len(trajectory_results)))\n            * (avg_coherence * 0.5 + avg_phi_score * 0.5),\n        )\n\n        logger.info(\n            \"Enhanced guidance: %d recommendations, %d warnings, confidence=%.2f\",\n            len(recommendations),\n            len(warnings),\n            confidence,\n        )\n\n        return EnhancedGuidance(\n            recommendations=recommendations,\n            warnings=warnings,\n            confidence=confidence,\n            similar_task_count=len(trajectory_results),\n            avg_coherence=avg_coherence,\n            avg_phi_score=avg_phi_score,\n            base_guidance=base_guidance,\n        )\n\n    def _generate_recommendations(self, successful: list[Any]) -> list[str]:\n        \"\"\"Generate recommendations from successful trajectories.\n\n        Args:\n            successful: List of successful TrajectorySearchResult\n\n        Returns:\n            List of recommendation strings\n        \"\"\"\n        if not successful:\n            return []\n\n        recommendations = []\n\n        # High-quality trajectories (coherence >= 0.7)\n        high_quality = [r for r in successful if r.coherence >= self.high_quality_threshold]\n        if high_quality:\n            # Sort by quality\n            high_quality.sort(key=lambda r: r.coherence * 0.5 + r.phi_score * 0.5, reverse=True)\n            best = high_quality[0]\n            recommendations.append(\n                f\"Approach similar to '{best.task_description[:50]}...' \"\n                f\"had excellent results (coherence={best.coherence:.2f}, \"\n                f\"phi={best.phi_score:.2f})\"\n            )\n\n        # Smooth trajectories (low variance)\n        smooth = [r for r in successful if r.trajectory_smoothness >= 0.7]\n        if smooth:\n            recommendations.append(\n                f\"{len(smooth)}/{len(successful)} similar tasks had smooth trajectories. \"\n                f\"Maintain steady progress through fabrics.\"\n            )\n\n        # Convergent trajectories (approach HIHO)\n        convergent = [r for r in successful if r.trajectory_convergence >= 0.7]\n        if convergent:\n            recommendations.append(\n                f\"{len(convergent)}/{len(successful)} similar tasks converged to HIHO. \"\n                f\"Target coherence ≈ 0.5 for stability.\"\n            )\n\n        return recommendations[:3]  # Top 3 recommendations\n\n    def _generate_warnings(self, failed: list[Any]) -> list[str]:\n        \"\"\"Generate warnings from failed trajectories.\n\n        Args:\n            failed: List of failed TrajectorySearchResult\n\n        Returns:\n            List of warning strings\n        \"\"\"\n        if not failed:\n            return []\n\n        warnings = []\n\n        # High failure rate\n        if len(failed) >= 3:\n            warnings.append(\n                f\"Warning: {len(failed)} similar tasks had poor outcomes. \"\n                f\"Proceed with caution.\"\n            )\n\n        # Low smoothness (chaotic trajectories)\n        chaotic = [r for r in failed if r.trajectory_smoothness < 0.3]\n        if chaotic:\n            warnings.append(\n                f\"{len(chaotic)} similar tasks had chaotic trajectories \"\n                f\"(high variance across fabrics). Plan carefully.\"\n            )\n\n        # Low convergence (didn't reach HIHO)\n        divergent = [r for r in failed if r.trajectory_convergence < 0.3]\n        if divergent:\n            warnings.append(\n                f\"{len(divergent)} similar tasks failed to converge. \"\n                f\"Monitor coherence closely.\"\n            )\n\n        return warnings[:2]  # Top 2 warnings\n\n    def to_dict(self, enhanced: EnhancedGuidance) -> dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Convert EnhancedGuidance to dictionary format.\n\n        Args:\n            enhanced: EnhancedGuidance instance\n\n        Returns:\n            Dictionary representation for executor\n        \"\"\"\n        return {\n            \"recommendations\": enhanced.recommendations,\n            \"warnings\": enhanced.warnings,\n            \"confidence\": enhanced.confidence,\n            \"similar_task_count\": enhanced.similar_task_count,\n            \"avg_coherence\": enhanced.avg_coherence,\n            \"avg_phi_score\": enhanced.avg_phi_score,\n            **enhanced.base_guidance,  # Merge in base guidance\n        }\n"
}

  "old_string": "    def get_experience_guidance(\n        self, task_description: str, project: str = \"cohezion\"\n    ) -> dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Fetch experience guidance from vault before execution.\n\n        Args:\n            task_description: Description of the task to execute\n            project: Project name for scoped search\n\n        Returns:\n            Dict with relevant_context (decisions, experiments, patterns)\n        \"\"\"\n        logger.info(\"Fetching experience guidance for: %s\", task_description)\n        result: dict[str, Any] = self.logger.get_experience_guidance(\n            task_description=task_description, project=project\n        )\n        return result",
  "new_string": "    def get_experience_guidance(\n        self, task_description: str, project: str = \"cohezion\", operation_type: str = \"generate\"\n    ) -> dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Fetch experience guidance from vault before execution.\n\n        Enhanced with trajectory search: finds similar past executions and\n        provides recommendations based on their outcomes.\n\n        Args:\n            task_description: Description of the task to execute\n            project: Project name for scoped search\n            operation_type: Type of operation (for trajectory search)\n\n        Returns:\n            Dict with relevant_context (decisions, experiments, patterns)\n            plus trajectory-based recommendations, warnings, and confidence\n        \"\"\"\n        logger.info(\"Fetching experience guidance for: %s\", task_description)\n\n        # Step 1: Get base guidance from vault\n        base_guidance: dict[str, Any] = self.logger.get_experience_guidance(\n            task_description=task_description, project=project\n        )\n\n        # Step 2: Enhance with trajectory search (if available)\n        try:\n            from cohezion.compound.trajectory_search import TrajectorySearchEngine\n            from cohezion.compound.guidance_enhancer import GuidanceEnhancer\n            from cohezion.flume.experience_collector import ExperienceCollector\n            from cohezion.flume.experience_encoder import ExperienceEncoder\n\n            # Initialize search components (lazy)\n            collector = ExperienceCollector()\n            encoder = ExperienceEncoder()\n            search = TrajectorySearchEngine(collector, encoder)\n            enhancer = GuidanceEnhancer()\n\n            # Find similar trajectories\n            trajectory_results = search.find_similar_trajectories(\n                task_description=task_description,\n                operation_type=operation_type,\n                top_k=5,\n                min_coherence=0.4,  # HIHO threshold\n            )\n\n            # Enhance guidance\n            enhanced = enhancer.enhance_guidance(base_guidance, trajectory_results)\n            result = enhancer.to_dict(enhanced)\n\n            logger.info(\n                \"Guidance enhanced with %d similar trajectories (confidence=%.2f)\",\n                enhanced.similar_task_count,\n                enhanced.confidence,\n            )\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            logger.debug(\n                \"Trajectory search failed (non-blocking): %s. Using base guidance only.\",\n                e,\n                exc_info=True,\n            )\n            result = base_guidance\n\n        return result"
}

  "content": "\"\"\"Tests for trajectory search engine (Phase 9).\"\"\"\n\nfrom unittest.mock import MagicMock\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport pytest\n\nfrom cohezion.compound.trajectory_search import (\n    TrajectorySearchEngine,\n    TrajectorySearchResult,\n)\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef mock_collector():\n    \"\"\"Create mock ExperienceCollector.\"\"\"\n    collector = MagicMock()\n\n    # Mock experiences with varying quality\n    collector.collect_all.return_value = [\n        {\n            \"task_description\": \"High quality task\",\n            \"operation_type\": \"generate\",\n            \"coherence\": 0.85,\n            \"phi_score\": 0.80,\n            \"trajectory_smoothness\": 0.75,\n            \"trajectory_convergence\": 0.70,\n            \"success\": True,\n        },\n        {\n            \"task_description\": \"Medium quality task\",\n            \"operation_type\": \"generate\",\n            \"coherence\": 0.60,\n            \"phi_score\": 0.55,\n            \"trajectory_smoothness\": 0.50,\n            \"trajectory_convergence\": 0.45,\n            \"success\": True,\n        },\n        {\n            \"task_description\": \"Low quality task\",\n            \"operation_type\": \"generate\",\n            \"coherence\": 0.35,\n            \"phi_score\": 0.30,\n            \"trajectory_smoothness\": 0.25,\n            \"trajectory_convergence\": 0.20,\n            \"success\": False,\n        },\n    ]\n    return collector\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef mock_encoder():\n    \"\"\"Create mock ExperienceEncoder.\"\"\"\n    encoder = MagicMock()\n\n    # Return deterministic vectors (similar tasks have similar vectors)\n    def encode_fn(exp):\n        # Base vector\n        vec = np.random.RandomState(42).randn(256)\n\n        # Modulate by task description (simulate semantic similarity)\n        if \"High quality\" in exp.get(\"task_description\", \"\"):\n            vec *= 1.0\n        elif \"Medium quality\" in exp.get(\"task_description\", \"\"):\n            vec *= 0.8\n        elif \"Low quality\" in exp.get(\"task_description\", \"\"):\n            vec *= 0.5\n        else:\n            vec *= 0.9  # Query tasks\n\n        return vec\n\n    encoder.encode_experience.side_effect = encode_fn\n    return encoder\n\n\nclass TestTrajectorySearchEngine:\n    \"\"\"Tests for TrajectorySearchEngine.\"\"\"\n\n    def test_find_similar_trajectories_returns_results(\n        self, mock_collector, mock_encoder\n    ):\n        \"\"\"Search returns similar trajectories ranked by quality.\"\"\"\n        search = TrajectorySearchEngine(mock_collector, mock_encoder)\n\n        results = search.find_similar_trajectories(\n            task_description=\"New high quality task\",\n            operation_type=\"generate\",\n            top_k=3,\n        )\n\n        assert len(results) > 0\n        assert isinstance(results[0], TrajectorySearchResult)\n        # First result should be highest quality (sorted)\n        assert results[0].coherence >= 0.5\n\n    def test_similarity_threshold_filters_low_matches(\n        self, mock_collector, mock_encoder\n    ):\n        \"\"\"Only results above similarity threshold are returned.\"\"\"\n        search = TrajectorySearchEngine(\n            mock_collector, mock_encoder, similarity_threshold=0.9\n        )\n\n        results = search.find_similar_trajectories(\n            task_description=\"Completely different task\",\n            operation_type=\"analyze\",\n            top_k=5,\n        )\n\n        # High threshold should filter most results\n        assert len(results) <= 3\n\n    def test_min_coherence_filters_poor_quality(\n        self, mock_collector, mock_encoder\n    ):\n        \"\"\"Tasks below min_coherence threshold are excluded.\"\"\"\n        search = TrajectorySearchEngine(mock_collector, mock_encoder)\n\n        results = search.find_similar_trajectories(\n            task_description=\"Test task\",\n            operation_type=\"generate\",\n            top_k=5,\n            min_coherence=0.5,  # Exclude low quality task\n        )\n\n        # Low quality task (coherence=0.35) should be filtered\n        assert all(r.coherence >= 0.5 for r in results)\n\n    def test_empty_experiences_returns_empty(self, mock_encoder):\n        \"\"\"Empty collector returns empty results gracefully.\"\"\"\n        empty_collector = MagicMock()\n        empty_collector.collect_all.return_value = []\n\n        search = TrajectorySearchEngine(empty_collector, mock_encoder)\n\n        results = search.find_similar_trajectories(\n            task_description=\"Any task\",\n            operation_type=\"generate\",\n            top_k=5,\n        )\n\n        assert results == []\n\n    def test_guidance_text_reflects_quality(\n        self, mock_collector, mock_encoder\n    ):\n        \"\"\"Guidance text varies based on trajectory quality.\"\"\"\n        search = TrajectorySearchEngine(mock_collector, mock_encoder)\n\n        results = search.find_similar_trajectories(\n            task_description=\"Test task\",\n            operation_type=\"generate\",\n            top_k=3,\n        )\n\n        # High quality result should have positive guidance\n        high_quality = next((r for r in results if r.coherence >= 0.7), None)\n        if high_quality:\n            assert \"excellent\" in high_quality.guidance.lower() or \"high confidence\" in high_quality.guidance.lower()\n\n        # Low quality result should have warning\n        low_quality = next((r for r in results if r.coherence < 0.5), None)\n        if low_quality:\n            assert \"caution\" in low_quality.guidance.lower() or \"failed\" in low_quality.guidance.lower()\n"
}

Mix of successful and failed trajectories\n    results = []\n\n    # 2 high-quality successful\n    for i in range(2):\n        result = MagicMock()\n        result.task_description = f\"High quality task {i}\"\n        result.operation_type = \"generate\"\n        result.coherence = 0.85\n        result.phi_score = 0.80\n        result.trajectory_smoothness = 0.75\n        result.trajectory_convergence = 0.70\n        result.similarity_score = 0.9\n        result.success = True\n        results.append(result)\n\n    # 1 medium-quality successful\n    result = MagicMock()\n    result.task_description = \"Medium quality task\"\n    result.operation_type = \"generate\"\n    result.coherence = 0.60\n    result.phi_score = 0.55\n    result.trajectory_smoothness = 0.50\n    result.trajectory_convergence = 0.45\n    result.similarity_score = 0.7\n    result.success = True\n    results.append(result)\n\n    # 1 failed\n    result = MagicMock()\n    result.task_description = \"Failed task\"\n    result.operation_type = \"generate\"\n    result.coherence = 0.30\n    result.phi_score = 0.25\n    result.trajectory_smoothness = 0.20\n    result.trajectory_convergence = 0.15\n    result.similarity_score = 0.6\n    result.success = False\n    results.append(result)\n\n    return results\n\n\nclass TestGuidanceEnhancer:\n    \"\"\"Tests for GuidanceEnhancer.\"\"\"\n\n    def test_enhance_guidance_generates_recommendations(\n        self, mock_trajectory_results\n    ):\n        \"\"\"Enhancer generates recommendations from successful trajectories.\"\"\"\n        enhancer = GuidanceEnhancer()\n        base_guidance = {\"decisions\": [], \"patterns\": []}\n\n        enhanced = enhancer.enhance_guidance(base_guidance, mock_trajectory_results)\n\n        assert isinstance(enhanced, EnhancedGuidance)\n        assert len(enhanced.recommendations) > 0\n        # Should mention high-quality tasks\n        assert any(\"excellent\" in rec.lower() or \"high\" in rec.lower() for rec in enhanced.recommendations)\n\n    def test_enhance_guidance_generates_warnings_from_failures(\n        self, mock_trajectory_results\n    ):\n        \"\"\"Enhancer generates warnings from failed trajectories.\"\"\"\n        enhancer = GuidanceEnhancer()\n        base_guidance = {\"decisions\": [], \"patterns\": []}\n\n        enhanced = enhancer.enhance_guidance(base_guidance, mock_trajectory_results)\n\n        # Should have at least one warning (1 failed task)\n        assert len(enhanced.warnings) > 0\n\n    def test_confidence_high_when_many_successful(\n        self, mock_trajectory_results\n    ):\n        \"\"\"Confidence is high when many high-quality results.\"\"\"\n        enhancer = GuidanceEnhancer()\n        base_guidance = {\"decisions\": [], \"patterns\": []}\n\n        enhanced = enhancer.enhance_guidance(base_guidance, mock_trajectory_results)\n\n        # 2 high-quality + 1 medium = good confidence\n        assert enhanced.confidence > 0.5\n\n    def test_empty_trajectories_returns_zero_confidence(self):\n        \"\"\"Empty trajectory list returns low-confidence guidance.\"\"\"\n        enhancer = GuidanceEnhancer()\n        base_guidance = {\"decisions\": [], \"patterns\": []}\n\n        enhanced = enhancer.enhance_guidance(base_guidance, [])\n\n        assert enhanced.confidence == 0.0\n        assert enhanced.similar_task_count == 0\n        assert enhanced.recommendations == []\n        assert enhanced.warnings == []\n\n    def test_to_dict_merges_base_guidance(\n        self, mock_trajectory_results\n    ):\n        \"\"\"to_dict() includes both enhanced and base guidance.\"\"\"\n        enhancer = GuidanceEnhancer()\n        base_guidance = {\"decisions\": [\"decision1\"], \"patterns\": [\"pattern1\"]}\n\n        enhanced = enhancer.enhance_guidance(base_guidance, mock_trajectory_results)\n        result_dict = enhancer.to_dict(enhanced)\n\n        # Should have both enhanced fields and base guidance\n        assert \"recommendations\" in result_dict\n        assert \"warnings\" in result_dict\n        assert \"confidence\" in result_dict\n        assert \"decisions\" in result_dict  # From base\n        assert \"patterns\" in result_dict  # From base\n        assert result_dict[\"decisions\"] == [\"decision1\"]\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/scripts/drivers/experience_guided_demo.py",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Demonstration of Phase 9: Experience-Guided Execution Loop.\n\nShows how the compound learning loop works:\n1. Execute task → Track trajectory\n2. Store in ExperienceCollector\n3. Query similar past trajectories\n4. Guide next execution with insights\n5. Better trajectory → Loop again\n\nThis creates exponential learning: every execution improves future executions.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport logging\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\n# Add src to path\nsys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent / \"src\"))\n\nfrom cohezion.compound.executor import CompoundExecutor\nfrom cohezion.compound.journey_tracker import JourneyTracker\nfrom cohezion.compound.inflection_detector import Severity\nfrom cohezion.flume.experience_collector import ExperienceCollector\nfrom unittest.mock import MagicMock\n\nlogging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format=\"%(name)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s\")\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\ndef create_mock_mcp():\n    \"\"\"Create mock MCP client.\"\"\"\n    client = MagicMock()\n    client.vault_find_relevant_context.return_value = []\n    client.vault_search.return_value = []\n    client.vault_write.return_value = \"success\"\n    client.vault_read.return_value = '{}'\n    return client\n\n\ndef create_mock_inflection(anomaly_score=0.1):\n    \"\"\"Create mock inflection detector.\"\"\"\n    detector = MagicMock()\n    anomaly = MagicMock()\n    anomaly.severity = Severity.INFO\n    anomaly.score = anomaly_score\n    anomaly.issues = []\n    anomaly.recommendations = []\n    anomaly.should_reexecute = False\n    detector.detect_anomaly.return_value = anomaly\n    return detector\n\n\ndef run_experience_guided_demo():\n    \"\"\"Run demonstration of experience-guided execution.\"\"\"\n    logger.info(\"=\" * 80)\n    logger.info(\"Phase 9: Experience-Guided Execution Loop Demo\")\n    logger.info(\"=\" * 80)\n\n    # Step 1: Initialize components\n    logger.info(\"\\n[1/5] Initializing components...\")\n    mock_mcp = create_mock_mcp()\n    journey_tracker = JourneyTracker(seed=42)\n    executor = CompoundExecutor(\n        mock_mcp,\n        enable_guardrails=False,\n        inflection_detector=create_mock_inflection(0.1),\n        journey_tracker=journey_tracker,\n    )\n    logger.info(\"✓ Executor initialized\")\n\n    # Step 2: Execute first task (no prior experience)\n    logger.info(\"\\n[2/5] Executing first task (cold start - no prior experience)...\")\n\n    def high_quality_task_1(guidance):\n        logger.info(f\"Task 1 guidance: {guidance.get('confidence', 0.0):.2f} confidence\")\n        return \"High quality output\", {\"quality\": 0.9}\n\n    result1 = executor.execute_task(\n        task_description=\"Generate creative solution\",\n        skill_name=\"creative_generation\",\n        operation_type=\"generate\",\n        execute_fn=high_quality_task_1,\n    )\n\n    logger.info(f\"✓ Task 1 complete: coherence={result1.metrics.get('coherence', 0.0):.3f}\")\n\n    # Step 3: Execute similar task (should get guidance from first task)\n    logger.info(\"\\n[3/5] Executing similar task (should receive guidance)...\")\n\n    guidance_received = {\"confidence\": 0.0}\n\n    def high_quality_task_2(guidance):\n        guidance_received[\"confidence\"] = guidance.get(\"confidence\", 0.0)\n        recs = guidance.get(\"recommendations\", [])\n        warns = guidance.get(\"warnings\", [])\n\n        logger.info(f\"Task 2 guidance: {guidance_received['confidence']:.2f} confidence\")\n        if recs:\n            logger.info(f\"  Recommendations: {len(recs)}\")\n            for rec in recs:\n                logger.info(f\"    - {rec[:80]}...\")\n        if warns:\n            logger.info(f\"  Warnings: {len(warns)}\")\n\n        return \"High quality output\", {\"quality\": 0.9}\n\n    result2 = executor.execute_task(\n        task_description=\"Generate another creative solution\",\n        skill_name=\"creative_generation\",\n        operation_type=\"generate\",\n        execute_fn=high_quality_task_2,\n    )\n\n    logger.info(f\"✓ Task 2 complete: coherence={result2.metrics.get('coherence', 0.0):.3f}\")\n\n    # Step 4: Execute low-quality task (creates negative experience)\n    logger.info(\"\\n[4/5] Executing low-quality task (creates warning for future)...\")\n\n    def low_quality_task(guidance):\n        logger.info(f\"Low quality task guidance: {guidance.get('confidence', 0.0):.2f} confidence\")\n        return \"\", {}  # Poor output\n\n    executor_low = CompoundExecutor(\n        mock_mcp,\n        enable_guardrails=False,\n        inflection_detector=create_mock_inflection(0.8),  # High anomaly\n        journey_tracker=journey_tracker,\n    )\n\n    result3 = executor_low.execute_task(\n        task_description=\"Generate risky solution\",\n        skill_name=\"risky_generation\",\n        operation_type=\"generate\",\n        execute_fn=low_quality_task,\n    )\n\n    logger.info(f\"✓ Task 3 complete: coherence={result3.metrics.get('coherence', 0.0):.3f} (low)\")\n\n    # Step 5: Execute similar risky task (should get warning)\n    logger.info(\"\\n[5/5] Executing similar risky task (should receive warnings)...\")\n\n    warnings_received = []\n\n    def risky_task_2(guidance):\n        warnings_received.extend(guidance.get(\"warnings\", []))\n        logger.info(f\"Risky task guidance: {guidance.get('confidence', 0.0):.2f} confidence\")\n        if warnings_received:\n            logger.info(f\"  Warnings received: {len(warnings_received)}\")\n            for warn in warnings_received:\n                logger.info(f\"    ⚠ {warn[:80]}...\")\n\n        return \"Cautious output\", {\"quality\": 0.5}\n\n    result4 = executor_low.execute_task(\n        task_description=\"Generate another risky solution\",\n        skill_name=\"risky_generation\",\n        operation_type=\"generate\",\n        execute_fn=risky_task_2,\n    )\n\n    logger.info(f\"✓ Task 4 complete: coherence={result4.metrics.get('coherence', 0.0):.3f}\")\n\n    # Summary\n    logger.info(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 80)\n    logger.info(\"EXPERIENCE-GUIDED LEARNING DEMONSTRATION\")\n    logger.info(\"=\" * 80)\n    logger.info(f\"Task 1: Cold start (no guidance) → coherence={result1.metrics.get('coherence', 0.0):.3f}\")\n    logger.info(f\"Task 2: Guided by Task 1 (confidence={guidance_received['confidence']:.2f}) → coherence={result2.metrics.get('coherence', 0.0):.3f}\")\n    logger.info(f\"Task 3: Low quality execution → coherence={result3.metrics.get('coherence', 0.0):.3f}\")\n    logger.info(f\"Task 4: Warned by Task 3 ({len(warnings_received)} warnings) → coherence={result4.metrics.get('coherence', 0.0):.3f}\")\n    logger.info(\"\\n✓ Experience-Guided Execution Loop demonstrated!\")\n    logger.info(\"Every execution improves guidance for future executions (exponential learning)\")\n\n    return True\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Entry point.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        success = run_experience_guided_demo()\n        sys.exit(0 if success else 1)\n    except Exception as e:\n        logger.exception(\"Demo failed:\")\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
}

{
  "command": "git commit -m \"feat: Phase 9 - Experience-Guided Execution Loop\n\n## Accomplishments\n\n**Phase 9: Experience-Guided Execution Loop** (Maximum compound engineering leverage)\n\nCreated exponential learning loop: every execution improves EVERY future execution.\n\n### Components Implemented:\n\n1. **TrajectorySearchEngine** (trajectory_search.py)\n   - Query ExperienceCollector using VAE embeddings\n   - Find similar past trajectories by semantic similarity\n   - Rank by combined score: similarity × (coherence + phi_score)\n   - Filter by HIHO threshold (min_coherence >= 0.4)\n\n2. **GuidanceEnhancer** (guidance_enhancer.py)\n   -
